## Known exceptions and evidence limits

- ADR/history/release-note/evidence/sketch paths remain historical/format exceptions. ADR 0005's current C03 basis is explicit in C8; its historical text remains unchanged apart from link repairs.
- `backend/migrations/README` bytes and all 16 assets are checked against baseline. Packaging README and release-note paths remain. Classifier/workflows/assertions are untouched; full CI is expected.
- Required documentary source links are routed to canonical owning chapters. Archived line-number evidence is pinned to the baseline commit; moved-file relative links are rebased. Old externally published blob/main URLs cannot all be preserved without forbidden redirect duplicates.
- Installed optional skill/config path consumers require separate bounded read-only inspection; repository search alone cannot prove absent external consumers. No private original/database/secret was used in this task.
